---
title: "@duxweb/lumi-ui API"
description: "@duxweb/lumi-ui 的全部公开方法、类型、参数和返回值。"
---

Native json-render integration and shadcn-style React components. 入口：`@duxweb/lumi-ui`、`@duxweb/lumi-ui/catalog`、`@duxweb/lumi-ui/charts`。共 524 个公开符号，由 TypeScript 入口自动生成。

## 函数

### applyLumiTheme

Applies a theme to the document (or `target`): its CSS variables and its colour mode, following the system for `system`. `dispose` restores what was there.

```typescript
applyLumiTheme(config: ThemeConfig, target?: HTMLElement): LumiThemeController
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `config` | `{ mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; }` | 是 | 创建 Lumi 上下文时使用的 Provider 与运行环境配置。 |
| `target` | `HTMLElement \| undefined` | 否 |  |

`config` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `brand` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `danger` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fontSize` | `"sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fontSizes` | `Partial<Record<"label" \| "page" \| "title" \| "caption" \| "body" \| "section" \| "metric", number>> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `info` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `mode` | `"light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `neutral` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `primary` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `radius` | `"sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `success` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `warning` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `LumiThemeController`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `config` | `{ mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; }` | 是 | 创建 Lumi 上下文时使用的 Provider 与运行环境配置。 |
| `dispose` | `() => void` | 是 |  |

### connectPromptInput

The PromptInput registry body. A host whose @ picker searches a backend
passes it here, e.g. Admin's resource-backed picker for `mentions.sources`.

```typescript
connectPromptInput(mentionPicker?: LumiPromptInputProps["mentionPicker"]): (context: Context<PromptInputProps>) => ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `mentionPicker` | `((props: PromptMentionPickerProps) => ReactNode) \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `(context: Context<PromptInputProps>) => ReactElement`。

### embeddableUrl

The address a frame may show, or undefined. A path on this site always
passes; another site needs https and, when `origins` is given, one of them.
`javascript:`, `data:`, `blob:` and every other scheme are refused.

```typescript
embeddableUrl(src: string, origins?: readonly string[]): string | undefined
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `origins` | `readonly string[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `string | undefined`。

### loadOutlineIcon

Resolves any HugeIcons free icon by name. The common set is bundled; any
other name loads the full icon package once, in its own chunk, so pages and
Specs can use icons the library never registered without bloating the entry.

```typescript
loadOutlineIcon(name?: string): Promise<OutlineIconComponent | undefined>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `name` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `Promise<OutlineIconComponent | undefined>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `$$typeof` | `symbol` | 是 |  |
| `displayName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Used in debugging messages. You might want to set it explicitly if you want to display a different name for debugging purposes. |
| `propTypes` | `any` | 否 | Ignored by React. |

### loadOutlineIconNames

Every HugeIcons free icon name, as `loadOutlineIcon` accepts it. Loads the full set once.

```typescript
loadOutlineIconNames(): Promise<string[]>
```

返回 `Promise<string[]>`。

### LumiActivityFeed

Who did what, newest first. The avatar leads, the sentence and the time
share the first line, and context sits under the sentence with the status
under the time, so badges line up in one column. Rows are plain
reading: nothing here navigates, so nothing reacts to the pointer.

```typescript
LumiActivityFeed(props: ActivityFeedProps & HostProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `{ items: { key: string; actor: string; action: string; avatar?: string \| undefined; target?: string \| undefined; context?: string \| undefined; status?: { label: string; tone?: "success" \| "brand" \| ... 5 more ... \| undefined; } \| undefined; timestamp?: string \| undefined; dateTime?: string \| undefined; }[]; ... 5 mo...` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `ariaLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `data-testid` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `empty` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `items` | `{ key: string; actor: string; action: string; avatar?: string \| undefined; target?: string \| undefined; context?: string \| undefined; status?: { label: string; tone?: "success" \| "brand" \| "warning" \| ... 4 more ... \| undefined; } \| undefined; timestamp?: string \| undefined; dateTime?: string \| undefined; }[]` | 是 |  |
| `loading` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiAgentMentionPicker

The @ reference picker. A search field, a tab per kind of thing, and the
results with the match highlighted. Up/Down move through the results and
wrap, Left/Right (at the edge of the search) switch the kind, as do Tab and
Shift+Tab with `cycleTabs`, and Enter picks. A pick becomes a context item
in `value`. Static options are filtered here; when the host searches
remotely it passes `onQueryChange`, `loading` and `hasMore`, and the list
shows what it is given.

```typescript
LumiAgentMentionPicker(props: LumiAgentMentionPickerProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `LumiAgentMentionPickerProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `ariaLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `cycleTabs` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Tab and Shift+Tab switch the kind (wrapping) instead of moving focus. Only for a picker inside a popover that Escape closes; standalone, Tab must leave the field. Defaults to true inside PromptInput's @ menu. |
| `data-testid` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `emptyText` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `hasMore` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | More results exist past the end; scrolling to the end asks for them. |
| `loading` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `loadingMore` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `maxHeight` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Pixels; the result list scrolls beyond it. |
| `multiple` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Allow several picks; otherwise a pick replaces the previous one. |
| `onChange` | `((value: AgentContextItem[]) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onLoadMore` | `(() => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Asked when the list is scrolled to its end while `hasMore`. |
| `onQueryChange` | `((query: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onSelect` | `((item: AgentContextItem, picked: boolean) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | A pick, with the item it added or removed. |
| `onTypeChange` | `((type: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `options` | `{ value: string \| number; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; avatar?: string \| undefined; image?: string \| undefined; type?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Results for the active tab. Without a remote source they are filtered by `type` and `query` here. |
| `placeholder` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `query` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The search text. Bind it to keep it in state. |
| `remote` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | The host searches; `options` are shown as given. |
| `type` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The active tab. Bind it to keep it in state. |
| `types` | `{ value: string; label: string; icon?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | The kinds of things to reference, shown as tabs, e.g. orders, articles, customers. |
| `value` | `{ id: string; label: string; kind?: "record" \| "file" \| "page" \| "selection" \| "data" \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; description?: string \| undefined; data?: Record<string, unknown> \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | The picked references as context items; bind it to the PromptInput `context` so each pick becomes a chip. |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiAgentPlan

The steps an agent intends to take, on the same timeline as its activity:
a summary line with the title and progress, then one row per step. The
running step shimmers, steps not started yet stay quiet, and a skipped or
failed step carries a status tag before its reason; the list folds from
the summary line.

```typescript
LumiAgentPlan(props: AgentPlanProps & HostProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `{ steps: { id: string; label: string; status: "done" \| "running" \| "pending" \| "failed" \| "skipped"; detail?: string \| undefined; }[]; title?: string \| undefined; className?: string \| undefined; 'data-testid'?: string \| undefined; 'aria-label'?: string \| undefined; } & HostProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `data-testid` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `steps` | `{ id: string; label: string; status: "done" \| "running" \| "pending" \| "failed" \| "skipped"; detail?: string \| undefined; }[]` | 是 |  |
| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiAgentQuestion

A question the agent asks before it goes on. A single choice answers as
soon as an option is pressed (digit keys pick by number); several choices
are ticked and confirmed; a written answer is sent with Enter. Answered or
skipped, it folds to one line with the answer, which opens to change it.

```typescript
LumiAgentQuestion(props: AgentQuestionProps & HostProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `{ title: string; status: "pending" \| "answered" \| "skipped"; description?: string \| undefined; mode?: "single" \| "text" \| "multiple" \| undefined; options?: { value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined; ... 7 more ...; 'aria-label'?: string \| undefined; } & { ...; } & HostProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `allowText` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `answer` | `string \| string[] \| null \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

### LumiAgentSessionList

A list of conversations. Every row keeps a dot slot before its title, so
titles line up whether or not a status shows; `detailed` adds the time and
the last line. Static sessions are filtered here; a host that searches
remotely passes `remote` and shows what it is given.

### LumiApproval

A confirmation before a registered action runs. While pending it is a card:
what will happen in the reader's words, then what it covers (items to
narrow, product lines, label and value rows and a total) and the decision.
`running` locks the buttons while the action is in flight, `failed` keeps
the card open with the reason. Once decided it folds to one line with the
outcome, which opens to show what was decided.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

### LumiChart

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-ui/charts`

Area, line and donut draw on the lazily loaded canvas engine; bars are
layout, solid rounded bars on a track measured against the largest reading,
so they need no engine. A bar is an amount, not progress: it never uses the
segmented progress meter. The readings
are always in the DOM: as text beside each bar, as the donut legend, and as
a screen-reader table for the canvas charts.

```typescript
LumiChart({ type, points, ariaLabel, height, color, valueFormat, locale, currency, emptyLabel, errorLabel }: LumiChartProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ariaLabel` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `color` | `2 \| 1 \| 4 \| 3 \| 5 \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `currency` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `dataLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

### LumiChatMessage

host's children, the citation chips, the actions and the follow-ups; the
message never mutates itself, it reports through the callbacks.

### LumiChatThread

A conversation from agent messages. When it is the scroll container it
follows new content while the reader is at the bottom, including content
that grows after render such as highlighted code or loaded images, and
stays put once they scroll up to read. Host children and the jump button
sit after the message list, not inside it.

### LumiDialog

The React component of the JSON `Dialog` element. A modal panel for a focused task or a confirmation; its width follows the content within the size range. Lead-in text belongs in description, which sits under the title in the header, not as the first child. Without children it is a compact confirmation sized to its title and description, led by a tone icon unless icon is false.

### LumiEmpty

Says why an area is empty and what to do next: a glyph on a soft tint, a
title, a quieter description and an optional way forward. `sm` stands in
for rows inside a table, card or panel; `md` for a whole page; `xs` is one
quiet line for option panels and narrow columns, so a dropdown stays short.

```typescript
LumiEmpty(props: LumiEmptyProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `LumiEmptyProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `data-testid` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `description` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Plain text in JSON; a React host may pass several lines. |
| `headingLevel` | `2 \| 4 \| 3 \| undefined` | 否 | Speaks the title as a heading of this level, for a state that stands in for a whole page or region. |
| `icon` | `string \| ReactElement<unknown, string \| JSXElementConstructor<any>> \| null \| undefined` | 否 | A HugeIcons name, or an icon element the host already holds. |
| `id` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 记录主键。 |
| `role` | `"status" \| "alert" \| undefined` | 否 | `status` makes the message a polite live region, `alert` an assertive one; omitted, it is plain content. |
| `size` | `"sm" \| "md" \| "xs" \| null \| undefined` | 否 | `sm` inside a table, card or panel; `md` for a whole page or region; `xs` one quiet line for dropdowns and narrow columns. |
| `title` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 | Why the area is empty, e.g. `No orders yet`. |
| `tone` | `"success" \| "warning" \| "danger" \| "info" \| "neutral" \| null \| undefined` | 否 | Tints the glyph: `danger` for a failure, `warning` for a blocked or offline state, `success` for a finished one. |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

message never changes the layout. It opens while the control is hovered or
focused, and once by itself when the error first appears.

### LumiForm

The React component of the JSON `Form` element. Groups fields, runs their `checks` and emits `submit` when they pass. In a JSON page the values live in page state, bound on each field, and business rules go to the `submitForm` action; `value`, `defaultValue` and `rules` here are for React hosts that submit the form themselves.

### LumiFragment

Grouping with no surface of its own. A bare React Fragment cannot carry the
protocol's `layout` props — the renderer refuses one that tries — so this
renders them: `stack` is a column, `split` puts the last child in an aside of
`asideWidth` and gives the rest the remaining measure.

```typescript

```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `asideWidth` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `data-testid` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `layout` | `"stack" \| "split" \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiGrid

The React component of the JSON `Grid` element. Grid layout (1-6 columns), compatible with the json-render shadcn Grid; drops columns as its own width shrinks (each column stays at least 12rem), so it also works inside drawers, cards and embedded previews. `minColumnWidth` (px) instead fits as many columns as the width allows. `align: "stretch"` gives the items of a row one height, as for a row of cards.

```typescript

```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiImage

The React component of the JSON `Image` element. An image in a fixed `aspectRatio` frame (default 4/3; `auto` keeps its own) with a loading shimmer and an image-off fallback when it fails. `radius` follows the theme (md by default). `preview` makes it a button that opens the full-screen Lightbox. `width` caps it in px; `caption` prints below and labels the preview.

```typescript
LumiImage(props: ImageProps & HostProps): ReactElement
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `{ src: string; alt?: string \| null \| undefined; aspectRatio?: "auto" \| "1/1" \| "4/3" \| "3/2" \| "16/9" \| "21/9" \| "3/4" \| null \| undefined; fit?: "cover" \| "contain" \| null \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; 'data-testid'?: string \| undefined; } & HostProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `alt` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `aspectRatio` | `"auto" \| "1/1" \| "4/3" \| "3/2" \| "16/9" \| "21/9" \| "3/4" \| null \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `caption` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 |  |

| `width` | `number \| null \| undefined` | 否 |  |

### LumiImageGrid

An image uploader laid out as square tiles over a bound `{ url }[]` list;
the first tile is the cover. Files come from the upload tile or a drop and
go through the same queue as `LumiUpload`, so each shows its own progress,
cancel and retry. Tiles reorder by pointer drag (a long press on touch) or
from the keyboard, the way Kanban cards do.

```typescript
LumiImageGrid(props: ImageGridProps & HostProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `{ value?: { url: string; thumbnail?: string \| undefined; alt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined; defaultValue?: { url: string; thumbnail?: string \| undefined; alt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined; ... 18 more ...; 'aria-label'?: string \| undefined; } & { ...; } & HostProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accept` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | File types the picker offers and a drop accepts; defaults to `image/*`. |
| `addLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Caption on the upload tile. |
| `appearance` | `"outline" \| "filled" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `aria-describedby` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `ariaLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `checks` | `{ type: string; message: string; args?: Record<string, string \| number \| boolean \| { $state: string; } \| null> \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `coverLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Caption on the first tile. |
| `data-testid` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `defaultValue` | `{ url: string; thumbnail?: string \| undefined; alt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `disabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `error` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `hint` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the generated "types up to size" line. |
| `id` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 记录主键。 |
| `invalid` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `loading` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `max` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Most images the grid holds; the upload tile disappears once it is reached. |
| `maxSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Largest accepted file in bytes. |
| `multiple` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | The picker and a drop take several files at once; defaults to true. |
| `name` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onCancel` | `(() => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onChange` | `((value: ImageGridItem[]) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onFiles` | `((files: File[]) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | For a host that uploads by itself: the accepted files, after which it writes `value`. |
| `onUpload` | `((files: File[]) => Promise<string[]>) \| undefined` | 否 | Uploads one batch and resolves its URLs; progress comes through `progress`. |
| `onUploadFile` | `((file: File, context: UploadFileContext) => Promise<string>) \| undefined` | 否 | Uploads one file with its own progress and cancellation; preferred over `onUpload`. |
| `progress` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `readonly` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `required` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `size` | `"sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `value` | `{ url: string; thumbnail?: string \| undefined; alt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiPromptInput

### LumiSegmentBar

A meter drawn as a row (or column) of rounded segments, lit from the start.
It is decoration: the reading belongs to the element that owns it, which
carries the number as text or an ARIA value.

### LumiSkeleton

The React component of the JSON `Skeleton` element. A placeholder in the shape of content that is still loading. `text` draws `lines` of natural widths, `circle` an avatar, `rect` a card or image, and `list-row` an avatar with two text lines per row. Shimmers unless the reader prefers reduced motion; `label` names the loading region.

### LumiTable

The React component of the JSON `Table` element. Rows and columns with sorting, selection and cell kinds such as badge, tags and currency. `rowActions` elements render in each row of the `kind: "actions"` column with their own `on`; props read the row via `$item`, params via the bound `activeRow`. Otherwise the column's `actions` menu emits `rowAction`. `highlightField` tints rows whose field is truthy. While rows are selected, `bulkActions` show a selection bar; `bulkAction` binds the pressed value to `activeAction`.

### LumiThinking

The work behind an answer as one quiet line: the current phase shimmering
with the elapsed time while working, then what it took once done. Steps
and reasoning fold out beneath it on a timeline: open while working,
folded once done unless the reader opened it, or `open` decides.

### LumiTimeline

The React component of the JSON `Timeline` element. Events in order on one continuous rule, each with a time, a status and optional detail under the title. The time sits at the end of the title's line (`datePlacement: "below"` stacks it under the title). Status markers: complete is a success dot, current a brand ring, upcoming a hollow ring. `marker: "dot"` draws an activity feed: a dot per item in its tone.

```typescript

```

### LumiUpload

File field over a bound list of URLs. Only URLs enter `value`; the files in
flight, and the ones that failed, live here until they finish or are dismissed.
`onUploadFile` gives each file its own progress, cancel and retry; a host
that only has `onUpload` sends a batch and reports one `progress`.

### rowActionsRenderer

Draws a table's `rowActions` slot once per row inside a native repeat scope.

- Props read the row through `$item` and `$index`, e.g. a label, `visible` or
  `disabled` per row.
- Before a control's own `on` runs, `activate` receives the row, so the
  table's bound `activeRow` already holds it: action params read it with
  `{ "$state": "/table/row/id" }`. Native `$item` in action params is a state
  path, not a value, and only exists when the rows were bound with
  `$bindState` (`rowsPath`).

```typescript
rowActionsRenderer(nodes: ReactNode, activate: (row: Record<string, unknown>) => void, rowsPath?: string): ((row: Record<string, unknown>, index: number) => ReactNode) | undefined
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `nodes` | `ReactNode` | 是 |  |
| `activate` | `(row: Record<string, unknown>) => void` | 是 |  |
| `rowsPath` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `((row: Record<string, unknown>, index: number) => ReactNode) | undefined`。

返回 `Record<string, string>`。

### TreeCheckMark

The tick drawn inside a checkable row; the row itself carries `aria-checked`.

```typescript
TreeCheckMark({ state, disabled }: { state: CheckState; disabled?: boolean | undefined; }): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `{ state: CheckState; disabled?: boolean \| undefined; }` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `disabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `state` | `CheckState` | 是 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### TreeLabel

A tree node's label with the searched text highlighted.

```typescript
TreeLabel({ label, needle }: { label: string; needle: string; }): ReactNode
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `{ label: string; needle: string; }` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `label` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `needle` | `string` | 是 |  |

返回 `ReactNode`。

### treeSubtree

The node and every loaded node below it, in document order.

```typescript
treeSubtree(node: TreeItem): TreeItem[]
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `node` | `CascaderOption` | 是 |  |

`node` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` | `CascaderOption[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `disabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `icon` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `isLeaf` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | With no `children`, a node that is not a leaf loads them through `onLoadChildren`. |
| `label` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `value` | `OptionValue` | 是 |  |

返回 `CascaderOption[]`。

### useBoundOrLocal

A bound prop writes through to the StateStore. An unbound one follows the
Spec until the reader changes it, then keeps that change locally. Exported
for registries that wrap these components (Admin's remote @ picker).

```typescript
useBoundOrLocal<T>(value: T | undefined, binding: string | undefined): [T | undefined, (next: T) => void]
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `T \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `binding` | `string \| undefined` | 是 |  |

返回 `[T | undefined, (next: T) => void]`。

### useOutlineIcon

Sync for bundled names; other names resolve after their first load.

```typescript
useOutlineIcon(name?: string): OutlineIconComponent | undefined
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `name` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `OutlineIconComponent | undefined`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `$$typeof` | `symbol` | 是 |  |
| `displayName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Used in debugging messages. You might want to set it explicitly if you want to display a different name for debugging purposes. |
| `propTypes` | `any` | 否 | Ignored by React. |

### useSelectionExtras

The commands a host added to selection bars; a table shows its bar for them even without bulk actions.

```typescript
useSelectionExtras(): readonly SelectionExtra[]
```

返回 `readonly SelectionExtra[]`。

### useUiText

The translate function and locale that UI components use for their own text: `t(key, fallback, params)`.

```typescript
useUiText(): UiText
```

返回 `UiText`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `locale` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `t` | `(key: string, fallback: string, params?: UiTextParams) => string` | 是 |  |
| `translate` | `UiTranslate` | 是 |  |

### LUMI_HIGHLIGHT_EVENT

The window event that flags rows for a moment, e.g. when an agent points at
them: `detail.keys` holds the row keys. Every table on the page with those
rows marks them and scrolls the first into view.

```typescript
const LUMI_HIGHLIGHT_EVENT: "lumi:highlight"
```

## 接口

### AgentMessageCallbacks

Callbacks a message hands to its parts, keyed by the message they belong to.

```typescript
interface AgentMessageCallbacks
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `onAnswer` | `((messageId: string, questionId: string, answer: AgentQuestionAnswer) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onApprovalEdit` | `((messageId: string, approvalId: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onApprovalSelect` | `((messageId: string, approvalId: string, selected: string[]) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onApprove` | `((messageId: string, approvalId: string, selected: string[]) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onCopy` | `((messageId: string, text: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onEditMessage` | `((messageId: string, text: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Edit a user message; the host decides what editing means, e.g. refill the prompt. |
| `onFeedback` | `((messageId: string, feedback: AgentFeedback) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onOpenSource` | `((source: AgentSourcePart, messageId: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onRegenerate` | `((messageId: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onReject` | `((messageId: string, approvalId: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onSkip` | `((messageId: string, questionId: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onSuggestion` | `((suggestion: AgentSuggestion, messageId: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |

### CascaderOption

A cascader node; `children` opens the next column.

```typescript
interface CascaderOption
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` | `CascaderOption[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `disabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `icon` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `isLeaf` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | With no `children`, a node that is not a leaf loads them through `onLoadChildren`. |
| `label` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `value` | `OptionValue` | 是 |  |

| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `command` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 | The last command picked without a `prompt`; bind it to read which one `command` emitted. |
| `commands` | `{ value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Slash commands, offered while the box holds only `/` and a name; arrow keys and Enter pick. |
| `context` | `{ id: string; label: string; kind?: "record" \| "file" \| "page" \| "selection" \| "data" \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; description?: string \| undefined; data?: Record<string, unknown> \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Records or pages referenced with |
| `data-testid` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `disabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `maxRows` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Rows the box grows to before it scrolls; defaults to 8. |
| `mentionable` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Shows the @ button, which emits `mention`. With `mentions` it opens the picker instead. |
| `mentionPicker` | `((props: PromptMentionPickerProps) => ReactNode) \| undefined` | 否 | Draws the @ picker, e.g. one that searches a backend; defaults to the static `mentions.options`. |
| `mentions` | `{ types?: { value: string; label: string; icon?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined; options?: { value: string \| number; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; avatar?: string \| undefined; image?: string \| undefined; type?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined; placeholder?: string \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | Turns on @ references: typing @ at the start of a word, or the @ button, opens the picker over the box, and a pick becomes a `context` chip. |
| `model` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `models` | `{ label: string; value: string; description?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Shows a compact model picker bound to `model`. |

| `onAttach` | `((files: File[]) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Without it the attach button is hidden; receives the picked files. |

| `onChange` | `((value: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onCommand` | `((command: AgentCommand) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | A slash command without a `prompt` was picked. |
| `onContextChange` | `((context: AgentContextItem[]) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onMention` | `(() => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onModelChange` | `((model: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onStop` | `(() => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onSubmit` | `((value: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Enter or the send button with non-empty text. |

| `tone` | `LumiSegmentTone \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `value` | `number` | 是 | Filled amount, from 0 to `max`. |

It reads in a monospace face and steps back, because nobody scans it for meaning; they scan it for a match. |

| `valueMap` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `width` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `wrap` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Prose such as a description or a note wraps inside the column instead of widening the table. Narrow tables keep it readable and scroll instead. |

| `value` | `string \| number \| boolean` | 是 |  |

### ActivityFeedItem

```typescript
type ActivityFeedItem = { key: string; actor: string; action: string; avatar?: string | undefined; target?: string | undefined; context?: string | undefined; status?: { label: string; tone?: "success" | "brand" | "warning" | ... 4 more ... | undefined; } | undefined; timestamp?: string | undefined; dateTime?: string | undefined; }
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `action` | `string` | 是 | What they did, e.g. `approved`. Read as `actor action target`; a language with another word order places `{actor}` and `{target}` in the phrase. |
| `actor` | `string` | 是 | Who did it: the avatar's initials and the sentence's first strong word. |
| `avatar` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Avatar image; without it the initials show. |
| `context` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Second line, e.g. the project or record it belongs to. |
| `dateTime` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Machine-readable time for the `<time>` element, e.g. an ISO date. |
| `key` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `status` | `{ label: string; tone?: "success" \| "brand" \| "warning" \| "danger" \| "info" \| "neutral" \| "primary" \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | A small badge on the second line, e.g. the outcome. |
| `target` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | What it was done to, in strong text. |
| `timestamp` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | When, as shown, e.g. `10 min ago` or `07:30`. |

### LumiNotifier

The notice queue. Its `open`/`close` pair has the shape of Lumi's
NotificationProvider, so a notifier is passed to `createLumi` as
`notificationProvider` and the `notify` and `closeNotice` actions land here.

```typescript
type LumiNotifier = LumiNotifier
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `clear` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `close` | `(key: string) => void` | 是 |  |
| `getSnapshot` | `() => readonly LumiNoticeEntry[]` | 是 |  |
| `open` | `(input: LumiNoticeInput) => string` | 是 |  |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 |  |

### LumiSegmentTone

```typescript
type LumiSegmentTone = LumiSegmentTone
```

### LumiToaster

The toast queue. Its `open`/`close` pair has the shape of Lumi's
ToastProvider, so a toaster is passed to `createLumi` as `toastProvider` and
the `toast` action and automatic error reports land here.

```typescript
type LumiToaster = LumiToaster
```

| `open` | `(input: LumiToastInput) => string` | 是 |  |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 |  |
